# Daily travel time by mode: Zahavi supplement

Monday–Friday. ENTD 2008 and EMP 2019; section 9.9. 200 independent household draws per survey are shared across scopes and statistics. Cells under 50 observations are suppressed.

Deciles linearly interpolate cumulative weights at distinct duration support points; step deciles are also shown. KS is a weighted distance, not a significance test. Threshold changes are percentage points.

Per-person values include days without trips as zero; open days are removed from numerator and denominator for round-trip scopes. Day-mode groups include walking plus the single non-walking mode; they are not the same estimand as mode-user days.

2008: 51.975 min/person; published scope-cube reference 52.0 min; matches.
2019: 57.032 min/person; published scope-cube reference 57.0 min; matches.

KDE bandwidths are pooled by mode and kind, with each survey given equal total weight; Gaussian densities are not boundary corrected. The complete minute grids are in `step12_modes/distributions.csv`.

## B3: quoted mode decomposition


ΔT = (N_B - N_A) . M_A + N_B . (M_B - M_A); the second term splits into mode mix Σ (s_B - s_A) . m_A, within Σ s_A . (m_B - m_A) and interaction Σ (s_B - s_A) . (m_B - m_A), all scaled by N_B into minutes per day. Household-bootstrap 95% CIs.
N, T and mean durations use all trips. Km/trip and speed use distance-known trips only; their weighted shares are shown. The within selection residual separates changes in the all-trip/known-distance mean-duration ratio from speed. A group with no usable distances has no km/speed split.

| Term | Minutes/day | 95% CI |
|---|---|---|
| ΔT | 5.2 | [3.7, 7.0] |
| trips volume | -2.6 | [-3.8, -1.5] |
| mode mix | 0.8 | [-0.1, 1.6] |
| within | 7.0 | [5.5, 8.9] |
| interaction | 0.0 | [-0.3, 0.3] |

Trips per day: 3.70 (2008) -> 3.55 (2019); minutes per trip: 17.3 -> 19.5.
